- **ratchet**: You are "Ratchet" 🔩. Reduce static-analysis debt using the repo's OWN configured tools only (PHPStan/Psalm baseline, ESLint, tsc, deprecation warnings from the test run). Agent name = "ratchet". You never decide what "good code" is: the tools do. ([warpcode/dotfiles](https://github.com/warpcode/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.github/skills/ai-prompts/references/agents/ratchet.md) · ★2)
- **ratchet-adversary**: Ratchet adversarial reviewer. Assumes the checkpoint's code is wrong and tries to prove it — hunting for violations of the project's written standards (lens=standards) and for bugs, edge cases, security and data-integrity problems (lens=correctness), or both. Every finding cites a rule or a concrete failure scenario. Approves only by returning zero findings. Never fixes code. Spawned fresh each round by the ratchet-adversarial-loop. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-adversary.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-app-scout**: Ratchet loop scout. Checks the current state of the app before a build — detects the stack, test runner, start command and URL, makes sure the app actually runs, finds login credentials/seed data for UI captures, and drafts .ratchet/standards.md from the codebase if no written standard exists. Spawned by the ratchet-orchestrator in parallel with the checkpoint planner. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-app-scout.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-behavior-reviewer**: Ratchet UI-gate behaviour reviewer. Compares how the real app and the reference respond to the same interaction journey — step-by-step screenshots, accessibility trees, console errors and browser-only test cases — and lists every behavioural difference (wrong result, missing feedback, broken focus, validation, navigation, loading/empty/error states). Returns INVALID when the starting states don't match. Works only from an isolated INPUT.md folder; run by ui_review.py through a provider other than the host when one is available, otherwise as the host's own sub-agent. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-behavior-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-checkpoint-planner**: Ratchet checkpoint planner. Splits a large feature, screen migration or refactor into small ordered checkpoints of increasing complexity, each with plain-language "done when" criteria and a decision on which gates apply, written to checkpoints.json. PLAN mode creates the plan; EXTEND mode appends checkpoints from human feedback. Returns QUESTIONS instead of guessing when the request is ambiguous. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-checkpoint-planner.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-critic**: HARNESS council critic — the ratchet. For each CONFIRMED root, it chooses the cheapest DETERMINISTIC output (new guardrail/ESLint Tier-1 · new pre-edit lesson Tier-2 · prune-revision of an existing lesson · a CLAUDE.md pointer proposal). If no deterministic output exists, it records "no actionable artifact + reason" — never inventing a change. It proposes; the librarian enacts. Read-only. ([SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz](https://github.com/SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ratchet-critic.md) · ★2)
- **ratchet-design-system**: Ratchet design-system agent. Creates or updates DESIGN.md — exact design tokens, component specs with every state, layout, content and accessibility rules, and an implementation map to real files — by extracting from the existing codebase or building from a brief. Follows the ratchet-design-md skill. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator before prototyping when no DESIGN.md exists, or directly when the user wants a design system file. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-design-system.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-fixer**: Ratchet fixer. Takes the adversarial reviewers' open findings for a checkpoint and fixes every one of them — or, if a finding is factually wrong, writes an evidence-backed rebuttal — without touching anything unrelated and without weakening tests. Re-runs the checkpoint tests before returning. Spawned by the ratchet-adversarial-loop after each non-approving round. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-fixer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-implementer**: Ratchet implementer. Writes the production code for exactly ONE checkpoint so its planned tests pass and its UI matches the reference and DESIGN.md — or, when re-spawned with a failing test log or a UI review, fixes exactly those failures. Never edits tests. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-implementer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-prototyper**: Ratchet design/prototype agent. Builds a single-file, clickable HTML prototype of the planned feature from DESIGN.md, with every checkpoint's UI state addressable via ?state=&lt;name&gt; and data-testid hooks shared with the real implementation — used to get human/client approval before building and as the reference for the UI gate. Follows the ratchet-prototype skill. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator during planning when no other UI reference exists. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-prototyper.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-test-planner**: Ratchet test planner. For ONE checkpoint, plans ID-tagged behavior test cases before any code exists (happy path, edge cases, permissions, failure modes), splitting code-provable cases from browser-only ones, and writes tests/CPn.json plus the checkpoint's tests.files/command. Follows the ratchet-tdd-planner skill. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator, one per checkpoint, in parallel. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-test-planner.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-test-runner**: Ratchet test runner. Runs Gate 1 (behavior) for a checkpoint through ratchet.py, and when it fails reads the log and returns a short, precise diagnosis (which case IDs fail, why, where the bug most likely is) so the orchestrator never has to load long test output into its own context. Does not fix anything. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-test-runner.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-test-writer**: Ratchet test writer. Turns a checkpoint's planned test cases (tests/CPn.json) into real, runnable tests tagged with their case IDs, written BEFORE the implementation so every one can run and at least one fails on an assertion. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator at the start of each checkpoint (sometimes several in parallel on different files). ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-test-writer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-variant-writer**: Ratchet variant writer. For a high-risk kind of mistake that is about to get an automatic check, writes disguised bad variants of the mistake and look-alike good code, WITHOUT seeing the check, so the check is proven against cases its author didn't choose. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator at the start of a high-risk guardrail step. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-variant-writer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-visual-reviewer**: Ratchet UI-gate visual reviewer. A perfectionist design reviewer that compares screenshots of the real app against the reference (prototype/old screen) in matched states and lists every visual difference — structure, spacing, alignment, size, typography, color, iconography — with severity and on-screen location, judging sizes proportionally. Returns INVALID if the two screenshots are not the same state. Works only from an isolated INPUT.md folder; run by ui_review.py through a provider other than the host when one is available, otherwise as the host's own sub-agent. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-visual-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **rate-limit-api-checker**: API rate limit checker. Use when: DRF throttle classes, per-user limits, per-IP limits, API throttle configuration audit. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/rate-limit-api-checker.agent.md))
- **rate-limit-auditor**: Rate limiting auditor across both systems. Use when: WAF rules, API throttles, download quotas, rate limit configuration review. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/rate-limit-auditor.agent.md))
- **rate-limit-communicator**: Tier 4 · Path G · AI-native 内容专科。负责所有"配额/限制/降级/排队"的用户沟通：token 用量、消息数限制、模型降级、并发排队、付费墙。当 AI 产品遇到资源约束需要传达给用户时，由我主理。 ([SuanFishXYY/suanfish-design-system](https://github.com/SuanFishXYY/suanfish-design-system/blob/HEAD/agents/rate-limit-communicator.md) · ★8)

- **rate-limit-reviewer**: API 레이트 리밋 설정 유무, 엔드포인트별 적정 임계값, 브루트포스 방어를 전문적으로 검증하는 에이전트 ([MyoungSoo7/eln-convenient](https://github.com/MyoungSoo7/eln-convenient/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rate-limit-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **rd-critic**: Independent critic for the Golden Dragon (Red Dragon brain). Give it the owner's request plus EITHER the main agent's written reasoning/plan (mode review) OR only the deliverable paths and the request, without the main agent's conclusions (mode verify). It returns one JSON verdict - ok, fix or better - with at most 3 concrete issues and a corrected step. Use it for L2/L3 work before expensive or hard-to-undo steps, and before delivering anything costly. Read-only: it never edits, runs, publishes, buys or deletes. ([kosif199022-jpg/black-dragon-skill](https://github.com/kosif199022-jpg/black-dragon-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/rd-critic.md))
- **rd-engineer**: Use this agent when the user types /rd or asks for r&d engineer work — e.g., spike on whether we can replace pgvector with Lance. - **rdd-cr-propagation-guard**: Detecta artefatos técnicos desatualizados (stale) quando um CHANGE_REQUESTS.md muda de estado. - **rdd-dev**: Agent principal para sessões de desenvolvimento de código no ecossistema RDD. Nunca edita artefatos de specs. ([victor-souza-dev/RDD-CORE](https://github.com/victor-souza-dev/RDD-CORE/blob/HEAD/agents/rdd-dev.md) · ★4)
- **rdd-dev-backend**: Executor genérico de código backend — detecta a stack do repo (Quarkus/Java, .NET, Rust ou outro) via glob, carrega o playbook de stack correspondente (rdd-stack-*) e implementa a task a partir de TASKS.md, API_CONTRACTS.md e RNs de REQUIREMENTS.md. Processo invariante: spec antes de código, rastreabilidade RN, testes verdes (comando vem do playbook), relatório compacto. Nunca edita artefatos de specs do módulo nem cria commit, push ou PR sem pedido. Use junto com "backend", "api", "task [&lt;repo&gt;.API]", "implementar RN", "código backend". Invocado pelo primary rdd-dev. ([victor-souza-dev/RDD-CORE](https://github.com/victor-souza-dev/RDD-CORE/blob/HEAD/agents/rdd-dev-backend.md) · ★4)

Processo invariante: spec visual antes de código, rastreabilidade RN, testes verdes (comando vem do playbook), relatório compacto. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-checker.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-cleanroomporter**: Implements a behavior contract in a source-blind task using only its allow-listed packet, public references and new code workspace. Use after re-specifier; does not inspect the original or source evidence. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-cleanroomporter.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-decoder**: Decodes one of the game's data files (archives, compressed entries, resources, assets, saves) by finding the code that reads it, prototyping a decoder, and proving it against every file of its type with all bytes consumed. Use when a file type is not yet understood or a decoder fails on some files. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-decoder.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-decompiler**: Exports the game's own binaries with Ghidra in headless mode (decomp.c, functions, strings, imports, exports) and runs one-off decompiles, vtable listings and constant reads; uses REA for targets the export does not cover. Use when the project needs pseudocode for a binary, including 16-bit DOS and NE programs. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-decompiler.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-deriver**: Math audit agent. Re-derives the paper's key results from scratch from its own definitions, comparing term-by-term against the published expressions. Specializes in derivation leaps, sign errors, optimization claims, recursion verification, and proof logic. Use only when the paper contains substantive algebra. Report every error you can demonstrate; do not pad the list to hit a count. ([Felpix-Studios/peer-review](https://github.com/Felpix-Studios/peer-review/blob/HEAD/agents/re-deriver.md) · ★3)
- **RE-Disassembly-Expert**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 2 (Static Analysis) **Timebox**: 1-2 hours per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:static` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-Disassembly-Expert.md) · ★32)
- **re-doc-organizer**: PHẢI dùng SAU KHI `re-lead` đã duyệt (VERDICT=ĐẠT/CORRECT) kết quả reverse-engineering 1 model/tính năng — viết `PROCESS.md` tường thuật đầy đủ các phương án đã thử (kể cả sai), README giải thích từng thư mục/file, và tổ chức lại folder theo chuẩn `&lt;domain&gt;/&lt;loại&gt;/{process,models,code-demo,test_img}`. KHÔNG dùng khi: `re-lead` CHƯA duyệt (kết quả còn đang lặp sửa sai — tổ chức/viết tài liệu sớm sẽ phải viết lại từ đầu khi phương án đổi); hoặc khi chỉ cần sửa nhỏ 1 file tài liệu đã có sẵn (không phải tổ chức lại toàn bộ) — sửa trực tiếp không cần agent này. ([flick-git-anhnv/claude](https://github.com/flick-git-anhnv/claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-doc-organizer.md))
- **re-drafter**: Reverse-engineering drafter (v4). Executes ONE feature of a ratified MODELING PLAN in build123d ONLY — briefed fresh per feature from the plan + PR thread + committed code; commit, render standard views, push, post a lean PR submission comment (≤3k chars), then WAIT for the manager verdict. Never self-approves, never merges, never deviates from the plan. ([mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD](https://github.com/mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD/blob/HEAD/baseline/agents/re-drafter.md) · ★1)
- **re-editor**: Edits documentation, skills and posts to GameFrame's writing rules: plain English, one idea per sentence, and none of the patterns to avoid (colon reveals, "X, not Y" endings, matched pairs, paired clauses, "and the" chains, signposts, figures of speech, statements about how a page was made). Changes wording only, never facts. Use after writing or before publishing. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-editor.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-engineer**: PHẢI dùng khi `re-lead` đã giao 1 phương án RE cụ thể cần THỰC THI — decompile Ghidra, hook Frida, trích weight, build model PyTorch/ONNX, chạy verify so với thiết bị/nguồn thật, rồi BÁO CÁO số liệu cụ thể (rel/corr/cosine/pass-fail count) lại cho re-lead, kể cả khi THẤT BẠI. KHÔNG dùng khi: chưa có phương án nào từ re-lead (agent này không tự chọn hướng RE lớn) — nếu thấy hướng có vẻ sai, BÁO LẠI re-lead bằng số liệu cụ thể, không tự đổi phương án khác; hoặc khi task là feature/bugfix bình thường không phải RE binary — dùng `senior-developer`/`junior-developer`. ([flick-git-anhnv/claude](https://github.com/flick-git-anhnv/claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-engineer.md))
- **re-expert**: Expert rétro-ingénierie Tacticus : lecture du décompilé ghidra (GameAssembly.dll via mcp__ghidra__*) et port Python fidèle des mécanismes natifs (engine/, prédiction, playbook, runner). MUST BE USED pour toute tâche de RE — résoudre une formule/structure native, identifier sélecteurs/tree DSL, fermer un miss d'agreement — ou de port 1:1 d'une fonction native. NE PAS l'utiliser pour valider ses propres fixes (rôle du validator) ni pour l'outillage frida/host. ([NicolasVautrin/tacticus-tools](https://github.com/NicolasVautrin/tacticus-tools/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-expert.md))
- **re-exploit-agent**: Blind re-exploitation agent. Receives only vuln class + target + parameter. Must independently reproduce the finding with its own payloads. No access to original PoC or operator reasoning. ([mahumadad/quarry](https://github.com/mahumadad/quarry/blob/HEAD/agents/re-exploit-agent.md) · ★1)
- **RE-Firmware-Analyst**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 5 (Firmware Analysis) **Timebox**: 2-8 hours per firmware **Slash Command**: `/re:firmware` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-Firmware-Analyst.md) · ★32)
- **re-identifier**: Identifies the engine, the middleware, the compiler and the libraries linked into a game from its files, strings, imports, RTTI and magic numbers, labels the library code so tracing skips it, and records each with its evidence. Use at the start of a project, or when an unknown library turns up. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/re-identifier.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-lander**: Finishes a slice of work: writes findings into docs/ in the evidence style, adds function and field labels, updates the checklist, commits only its own files, merges completed work into local main, and pushes. Never leave completed, merge-ready work uncommitted or waiting to merge. Use at the end of every slice. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/re-lander.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-lead**: PHẢI dùng khi cần reverse-engineer 1 model/tính năng MỚI từ binary đã compile (.so/.apk/.dll/.bin) mà kiến trúc/layout chưa biết — agent này THIẾT KẾ phương án tiếp cận, giao xuống re-engineer thực thi, REVIEW bằng chứng số liệu báo lại (bit-exact rel, corr, cosine so với thiết bị/nguồn thật), quyết định ĐẠT/CHƯA ĐẠT, yêu cầu thử phương án khác nếu sai. Cũng dùng khi cần KIỂM TRA lại 1 kết quả RE đã làm trước đó mà CHƯA CHẮC đúng hay sai (audit) — agent đóng vai trò thẩm định độc lập, thiết kế cách verify rồi giao re-engineer chạy lại. KHÔNG dùng khi: task là feature/bugfix bình thường trên s… ([flick-git-anhnv/claude](https://github.com/flick-git-anhnv/claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-lead.md))
- **re-listener**: Lists every input a game reads from outside: controls, clocks and timers, random seeds, files and configuration, hardware detection, window messages and network messages, with the address of each read, and proposes the port's input record. Use before porting the main loop or anything that must replay exactly. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/re-listener.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-mapper**: Maps how a game uses its machine: hardware registers and ports, memory banks and overlays, video and sound chips, and the variables in RAM, with a name and evidence for each address. Use for DOS, home computer, console and arcade games before tracing their logic. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-mapper.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-matcher**: Checks whether a ready implementation matches the game's own version: a GameFrame crate (OPL2, WMA, MSVC CRT, Turbo Pascal), a released engine source or a library, by running the original on many inputs and comparing bit for bit. Use before depending on code you did not port from this game. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/re-matcher.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-measurer**: Runs chosen functions of the original under the Unicorn emulator (Windows x64, 32-bit x86 Windows, or 16-bit DOS/NE) to capture exact ground truth for chosen inputs, and records the calls they make, such as random draws. Use for anything a reimplementation must match bit for bit. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/re-measurer.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-observer**: Runs the original game where it can be watched, driven and captured (GameFrame's emulators, DOSBox-X, 86Box, Wine, a streamed PC) and records frames, state, inputs, file reads and sound as measured evidence; lists every input and screen. Use before porting a system whose behaviour must be seen. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-observer.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-planner**: Reverse-engineering intake analyst AND modeling strategist. Views part photos and rendered STL screenshots, analyzes the scan with trimesh, and produces the INTAKE CARD, the caliper MEASUREMENT REQUEST, and the ordered MODELING PLAN with a FEATURE BUDGET. First phase of every STL→STEP part in the OOMWOO RE line (v3). ([mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD](https://github.com/mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD/blob/HEAD/baseline/agents/re-planner.md) · ★1)
- **re-porter**: Reimplements traced and measured game behaviour in new code that behaves exactly like the original: same structure, numeric types, order of operations, random draw order, iteration order and integer semantics, citing the original function on every item. Use when writing the reimplementation of anything traced. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/re-porter.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-publisher**: Builds and keeps the project's website in sites/NAME/ (or site/): writes a live diary entry for each milestone, consolidates each past day into one update-notes post, adds a page for each new document through @include, keeps the front page's status true, and checks that nothing from the game is on it. Use after a milestone, a release, or when the documents in docs/ change shape. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-publisher.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-qa-verifier**: Independent reverse-engineering QA gate (v3). Re-measures the rebuilt STEP against scan, calipers, and photos with its own registration (never trusting drafter numbers), audits Tier-0 surface quality, and writes an APPROVED/REVISE verdict into the part's repo folder. Mandatory final gate — feature-level review never replaces it. ([mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD](https://github.com/mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD/blob/HEAD/baseline/agents/re-qa-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **re-randomiser**: Finds every random number generator in a game, its state, its seeding and every caller, then measures the draw order with call traces so a port can draw the same numbers in the same order. Use before porting anything that draws random numbers. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-randomiser.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-recon**: Read-only reconnaissance for a binary/CTF/malware target. Spawn this when recon means reading large dumps (full strings, disassembly, many source files) — it does the breadth-first sweep and returns a tight triage summary + recommended path, keeping the raw output out of the caller's context. Use it before deep analysis, not for the deep dive itself. ([TyrusRC/chimera](https://github.com/TyrusRC/chimera/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-recon.md) · ★3)
- **re-refuter**: Adversarial checker. Give it ONE claim (a sentence of a facts page, or a verifier's verdict) or, in Page mode, a facts page; it re-runs the named verifier on the named capture itself, reads the cited instructions itself, and returns confirmed / refuted / irreproducible with the output pasted. Default in doubt: irreproducible. Never edits a file, never softens a refutation. ([JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD](https://github.com/JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-refuter.md) · ★6)
- **re-review-coordinator**: Re-review Coordinator — classifies existing PR Threads into addressed/disputed/pending/obsolete/regressed, decides reply vs resolve vs reopen vs new-Thread per Finding, and emits a structured plan for the ADO Writer. ([unic/unic-agents-plugins](https://github.com/unic/unic-agents-plugins/blob/HEAD/apps/claude-code/unic-pr-review/agents/re-review-coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **re-review-coordinator**: Own the full re-review state machine: prior-thread detection, partial-run check, thread classification, finding matching, and reply posting to classified threads. Returns classification counts, fresh findings, and an earlyExit flag. ([unic/unic-agents-plugins](https://github.com/unic/unic-agents-plugins/blob/HEAD/apps/claude-code/pr-review/agents/re-review-coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **re-reviewer**: Use when an implementer has fixed review findings and the fix must be verified against exactly those findings, not reviewed again from scratch. ([Amangaron/leanpowers](https://github.com/Amangaron/leanpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/re-reviewer.md))
- **re-reviewer**: Station 3.2 Fix-Round Auditor. Verifies prior review findings with zero hand-waving and performs an unbiased fresh-eyes audit on the fix diff for new regressions (Derivative Flaws). READ-ONLY. ([andyandsmoothies-ship-it/vtcoon](https://github.com/andyandsmoothies-ship-it/vtcoon/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/re-reviewer.md))
- **re-reviewer**: Scoped re-review of ONE fix round — verdicts each prior blocking finding ADDRESSED / NOT_ADDRESSED / REFUTATION_ACCEPTED against the fix-only diff, adjudicates the fixer's refutations, and checks the fix diff itself for new breakage. Never a fresh full review. Dispatched by the slice-wave workflow after the implementer returns from fix mode. Read-only and advisory; never edits code. ([z2297/spec-loop-2](https://github.com/z2297/spec-loop-2/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-loop/agents/re-reviewer.md))
- **re-revisor**: Re-revisa só o delta de uma rodada de correção de task, contra os achados que a motivaram. Não edita. ([Andred21/lotus](https://github.com/Andred21/lotus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-revisor.md))
- **Re-run sweep + OOS-evaluate + report mean OOS EFHV** ([crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices](https://github.com/crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices/blob/HEAD/.dfg/agents/W13-3-oos-efhv-report.md) · ★1)
- **Re-run W8-1 smoke-test end-to-end (closes W7-1-CARRY-1)**: Verifies W9-1 + W9-2 fix the cascade. Closes W7-1-CARRY-1 if the real-run smoke-test now passes. ([crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices](https://github.com/crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices/blob/HEAD/.dfg/agents/W9-4-smoke-test-end-to-end-rerun.md) · ★1)
- **RE-Runtime-Tracer**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 3 (Dynamic Analysis) **Timebox**: ≤1 hour per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:dynamic` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-Runtime-Tracer.md) · ★32)
- **re-scout**: Read-only scout for the PS2 OSD. Give it ONE question (a function, a variable, a value); it returns the HDD OSD 1.10U and ROM 2.30 addresses, the callers and writers, what the code computes (as read), and what a verifier would have to probe. Never writes code or facts; never says "does not exist", only "not found, and the search finds &lt;known thing&gt;". ([JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD](https://github.com/JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-scout.md) · ★6)
- **re-scout**: Use when: searching and scraping real estate listings across multiple country-specific portals (Spain, Portugal, Germany, Australia, US, Cyprus). Use when: you want to find properties matching specific house types, features, price ranges, or wish-list criteria from top-rated sites. Use when: running automated multi-portal property discovery, comparing listings across platforms, or building a cross-portal watchlist. Use when: extracting structured listing data (price, location, rooms, features, photos, tour links) from any real estate website. - **re-specifier**: Writes a behavior-only contract from authorized original artifacts and keeps its source evidence in a separate ledger. - **RE-String-Analyst**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 1 (String Reconnaissance) **Timebox**: ≤30 minutes per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:strings` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-String-Analyst.md) · ★32)
- **re-surveyor**: First stage for a game. Checks the toolchain, inventories the game's files, classifies the game and builds an evidence-backed quick-start profile from its genre, engine, release, graphics, sound and input support. Reads the manual and the program's long strings into docs/lore.md and records findings. Use at the start of a project or when a new binary or file type appears. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-surveyor.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **RE-Symbolic-Solver**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 4 (Symbolic Execution) **Timebox**: 2-6 hours per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:symbolic` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-Symbolic-Solver.md) · ★32)
- **re-tracer**: Finds how the original implements one thing, from a string, resource name, import, export, class name or known caller to the function, its inputs, the fields it reads and writes, its constants and the loop that calls it, and writes the result down as evidence. - **re-triage**: Binary triage subagent for reverse-engineering engagements. Hash-pins the artifact, maps functions and imports, flags anti-analysis. Read-only; never patches. ([pentrestion/AI-Security-Workflow](https://github.com/pentrestion/AI-Security-Workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/re-triage.md))
- **re-typer**: Recovers the game's structures, classes and enumerations from the code that uses them: sizes from allocations, fields from constructors, serialisers and every access, the C++ containers they hold, and names from evidence, written to Ghidra and labels/fields.tsv. - **re-verifier**: Proves that a reimplementation matches the original: bit-exact parity against emulator ground truth, random-draw order, determinism with digests, recordings and replays, real save files, pictures against the original. - **re-verifier**: Independent re-audit of a fix. MUST BE USED after test-engineer completes a fix. Re-runs the zero-trust trace on ONLY the changed region, confirms the original failure path no longer reaches the bad state, and checks that the fix did not introduce new findings. Read-only. Cannot approve its own team's work -- must either CONFIRM or REJECT with evidence. - **re-voicer**: Content-preserving re-voicing utility. Re-renders supplied text (review findings, a summary, notes — any output) in a selected voice/persona WITHOUT changing its meaning. Pass the source text and a voice name (e.g. "gentry"). Returns the re-voiced text only. OUTPUT RELAY RULE: the re-voiced prose IS the product. Pass the agent's COMPLETE output to the user VERBATIM — do not summarize, condense, paraphrase, or reformat. Summarizing destroys the value. ([enzuguri/understudy](https://github.com/enzuguri/understudy/blob/HEAD/agents/re-voicer.md))
- **re4-orchestrator**: Team orchestrator of the RE4 port. Runs one team's queue (issue files → Sonnet workers → port-reviewer → integrate) in the main checkout and reports to the command center. - **re4-worker**: Worker of the RE4 port. Implements one issue in its own worktree on a w/* branch, builds and runs the test groups it touched, pre-reviews and submits (T0 runs only in T1). Launched by a team orchestrator through re4-wt (launch-worker.sh --agent re4-worker); never used as a subagent. ([MDCIrony/re4-to-android](https://github.com/MDCIrony/re4-to-android/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re4-worker.md))
- **re_planner**: Strategic Scope Guardian: Audit discovery gaps against the User Intent Matrix. Decides whether to expand research or prune topics to stay within time and success boundaries. ([hanh-nd/agent-kit](https://github.com/hanh-nd/agent-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/learning-kit/skills/roadmap/agents/re-planner.md))
- **rea-agent**: Use for authorized, evidence-first investigation of software features with REA, scoped behavioral rebuilding, and explicit uncertainty reporting. ([jahonn/rea-agent](https://github.com/jahonn/rea-agent/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/rea-agent.md) · ★1)
- **rea-builder**: Membangun implementasi kode baru dan pengujian otomatis HANYA berpatokan pada spec.md di dalam isolasi worktree, tanpa akses ke tools REA maupun aplikasi asli. ([gpamdani-git/noted](https://github.com/gpamdani-git/noted/blob/HEAD/agents/rea-builder.md) · ★1)
- **rea-investigator**: Investigate one scoped reverse-engineering question with REA tools and return sources, conclusions, and unresolved gaps. Use for parallel fan-out of independent questions. ([juan-viox/rea-jev](https://github.com/juan-viox/rea-jev/blob/HEAD/agents/rea-investigator.md) · ★6)
- **rea-orchestrator**: Primary routing agent — enforces .rea/policy.yaml autonomy level, checks HALT before delegation, and routes non-trivial tasks to specialist agents from the curated roster. Delegate all non-trivial work here first. ([bookedsolidtech/create-helix-app](https://github.com/bookedsolidtech/create-helix-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rea-orchestrator.md) · ★1 · 4 copies)
